# 🚀 THANDIZO Production Deployment Guide

This guide will walk you through deploying THANDIZO to production so it's publicly accessible.

---

## 📋 Prerequisites

Before deploying, you'll need:

1. **GitHub Account** — To push your code
2. **Vercel Account** — For hosting (free tier available)
3. **Supabase Account** — For database + authentication (free tier available)
4. **Domain Name** (optional) — For custom branding

---

## 🎯 Deployment Overview

We'll deploy in this order:

1. **Push code to GitHub**
2. **Set up Supabase** (database + auth)
3. **Deploy to Vercel** (hosting)
4. **Configure environment variables**
5. **Test production deployment**
6. **(Optional) Add custom domain**

---

## Step 1: Push Code to GitHub

### 1.1 Create a GitHub Repository

1. Go to [github.com/new](https://github.com/new)
2. Repository name: `thandizo`
3. Make it **Public** or **Private** (your choice)
4. **Don't** initialize with README, .gitignore, or license
5. Click **Create repository**

### 1.2 Push Your Code

Run these commands in your terminal:

```bash
cd /home/user/thandizo

# Initialize git (if not already done)
git init

# Add all files
git add .

# Commit
git commit -m "🎉 Initial commit: THANDIZO Phase 1 Foundation"

# Add GitHub as remote
git remote add origin https://github.com/YOUR_USERNAME/thandizo.git

# Push to GitHub
git branch -M main
git push -u origin main
```

Replace `YOUR_USERNAME` with your GitHub username.

### 1.3 Verify on GitHub

Visit your repository at `https://github.com/YOUR_USERNAME/thandizo` and confirm all files are there.

---

## Step 2: Set Up Supabase

Supabase provides PostgreSQL database + authentication + file storage.

### 2.1 Create Supabase Account

1. Go to [supabase.com](https://supabase.com)
2. Click **Start your project**
3. Sign up with GitHub (easiest) or email

### 2.2 Create a New Project

1. Click **New Project**
2. Select your organization
3. Fill in project details:
   - **Name:** `thandizo`
   - **Database Password:** (generate a strong password and save it)
   - **Region:** Choose closest to your users (e.g., `West EU` for Africa)
   - **Pricing Plan:** Free (sufficient for starting)
4. Click **Create new project**
5. Wait 2-3 minutes for setup to complete

### 2.3 Get Your Credentials

1. Go to **Project Settings** → **API**
2. Copy these values:
   - **Project URL** (e.g., `https://xxxxx.supabase.co`)
   - **anon public key** (starts with `eyJ...`)
   - **service_role key** (starts with `eyJ...`)

3. Go to **Project Settings** → **Database** → **Connection string**
4. Copy the **Transaction** connection string (starts with `postgresql://...`)

**Save all these credentials securely!**

### 2.4 Push Database Schema

Run these commands locally:

```bash
cd /home/user/thandizo

# Install Supabase CLI (if not installed)
npm install -g supabase

# Log in to Supabase
supabase login

# Link your project (you'll need your project reference ID)
supabase link --project-ref YOUR_PROJECT_REF

# Push the schema
supabase db push
```

Your project reference ID is in the Supabase dashboard URL: `https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/YOUR_PROJECT_REF`

### 2.5 Verify Database

1. Go to Supabase Dashboard → **Table Editor**
2. You should see tables like `User`, `PatientApplication`, `Campaign`, etc.

---

## Step 3: Deploy to Vercel

Vercel is the easiest way to deploy Next.js applications.

### 3.1 Create Vercel Account

1. Go to [vercel.com](https://vercel.com)
2. Click **Sign Up**
3. Sign up with GitHub (easiest)

### 3.2 Import Your Project

1. Click **Add New...** → **Project**
2. Find your `thandizo` repository
3. Click **Import**

### 3.3 Configure Environment Variables

In the Vercel deployment screen, add these environment variables:

```bash
# Database
DATABASE_URL="postgresql://..."  # Your Supabase connection string

# Supabase
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL="https://xxxxx.supabase.co"
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY="eyJ..."

# App
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL="https://thandizo.vercel.app"  # Will update after deployment
```

### 3.4 Deploy

1. Click **Deploy**
2. Wait 2-3 minutes for build to complete
3. You'll get a URL like `https://thandizo-xxxxx.vercel.app`

### 3.5 Update Environment Variables

1. Go to your Vercel project dashboard
2. Go to **Settings** → **Environment Variables**
3. Update `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` with your actual Vercel URL
4. Click **Save**
5. **Redeploy** (Vercel will prompt you)

### 3.6 Test Your Deployment

1. Visit your Vercel URL
2. Test the landing page
3. Test the application form at `/start`
4. Check that everything works

---

## Step 4: Configure Authentication (Optional but Recommended)

If you want users to register and log in:

### 4.1 Enable Email Authentication in Supabase

1. Go to Supabase Dashboard → **Authentication** → **Providers**
2. Enable **Email**
3. Configure:
   - **Enable Email provider:** ✅
   - **Confirm email:** ✅ (recommended)
   - **Secure email change:** ✅
   - **Secure password change:** ✅

### 4.2 Configure Email Templates

1. Go to **Authentication** → **Email Templates**
2. Customize templates for:
   - Confirmation email
   - Password reset
   - Magic link (optional)

### 4.3 Add Site URL

1. Go to **Authentication** → **URL Configuration**
2. Add your Vercel URL to **Site URL**
3. Add to **Redirect URLs**

---

## Step 5: Set Up File Storage (For Document Uploads)

### 5.1 Create Storage Buckets

1. Go to Supabase Dashboard → **Storage**
2. Create two buckets:
   - **medical-documents** (private) — For medical reports, IDs
   - **campaign-photos** (public) — For campaign images

### 5.2 Configure Bucket Policies

**For medical-documents (private):**
```sql
-- Only authenticated users can upload
CREATE POLICY "Authenticated users can upload"
ON storage.objects FOR INSERT
TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (bucket_id = 'medical-documents');

-- Only document owners can view
CREATE POLICY "Users can view own documents"
ON storage.objects FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (bucket_id = 'medical-documents' AND owner = auth.uid());
```

**For campaign-photos (public):**
```sql
-- Anyone can view
CREATE POLICY "Public read access"
ON storage.objects FOR SELECT
TO public
USING (bucket_id = 'campaign-photos');

-- Authenticated users can upload
CREATE POLICY "Authenticated users can upload"
ON storage.objects FOR INSERT
TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (bucket_id = 'campaign-photos');
```

---

## Step 6: Configure Payment Gateways (When Ready)

### 6.1 Airtel Money API

1. Apply for Airtel Money API access
2. Get API credentials
3. Add to Vercel environment variables:
   ```bash
   AIRTEL_MONEY_API_KEY="..."
   AIRTEL_MONEY_API_SECRET="..."
   ```

### 6.2 MTN Mobile Money API

1. Apply for MTN MoMo API access
2. Get API credentials
3. Add to Vercel environment variables:
   ```bash
   MTN_MONEY_API_KEY="..."
   MTN_MONEY_API_SECRET="..."
   ```

### 6.3 Stripe (For Card Payments)

1. Create Stripe account at [stripe.com](https://stripe.com)
2. Get API keys from Dashboard → **Developers** → **API keys**
3. Add to Vercel environment variables:
   ```bash
   STRIPE_SECRET_KEY="sk_test_..."
   NEXT_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY="pk_test_..."
   ```

**Note:** Use test keys initially, switch to live keys when ready for real payments.

---

## Step 7: Add Custom Domain (Optional)

### 7.1 Purchase a Domain

Buy from:
- [Namecheap](https://namecheap.com)
- [GoDaddy](https://godaddy.com)
- [Google Domains](https://domains.google)

Recommended: `thandizo.com` or `thandizo.org`

### 7.2 Add Domain to Vercel

1. Go to Vercel project → **Settings** → **Domains**
2. Add your domain (e.g., `thandizo.com`)
3. Vercel will provide DNS records

### 7.3 Configure DNS

Add these records to your domain registrar:

**For root domain (@):**
```
Type: A
Name: @
Value: 76.76.21.21
```

**For www:**
```
Type: CNAME
Name: www
Value: cname.vercel-dns.com
```

### 7.4 Wait for DNS Propagation

DNS changes can take 5 minutes to 48 hours. Usually completes within 30 minutes.

### 7.5 Update Environment Variables

1. Go to Vercel → **Settings** → **Environment Variables**
2. Update `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` to your custom domain
3. Redeploy

---

## Step 8: Configure Email Notifications (Optional)

### 8.1 Use Resend (Recommended)

1. Sign up at [resend.com](https://resend.com) (free tier: 100 emails/day)
2. Verify your domain
3. Get API key from **API Keys** section
4. Add to Vercel environment variables:
   ```bash
   RESEND_API_KEY="re_..."
   ```

### 8.2 Alternative: SendGrid

1. Sign up at [sendgrid.com](https://sendgrid.com)
2. Get API key
3. Add to environment variables:
   ```bash
   SENDGRID_API_KEY="SG..."
   ```

---

## Step 9: Set Up Monitoring & Analytics

### 9.1 Vercel Analytics (Built-in)

1. Go to Vercel project → **Analytics**
2. Enable analytics (free for hobby plan)
3. View page views, performance metrics

### 9.2 Error Tracking (Recommended)

**Option A: Sentry**
1. Sign up at [sentry.io](https://sentry.io) (free tier available)
2. Create project for Next.js
3. Get DSN (Data Source Name)
4. Add to environment variables:
   ```bash
   NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN="https://..."
   ```

**Option B: LogRocket**
1. Sign up at [logrocket.com](https://logrocket.com)
2. Get app ID
3. Add to environment variables:
   ```bash
   NEXT_PUBLIC_LOGROCKET_ID="..."
   ```

### 9.3 Uptime Monitoring

Use [UptimeRobot](https://uptimerobot.com) (free):
1. Sign up
2. Add your domain
3. Get alerts when site is down

---

## Step 10: Production Checklist

Before announcing your platform, verify:

### ✅ Functionality
- [ ] Landing page loads correctly
- [ ] Application form works end-to-end
- [ ] All links work
- [ ] Mobile responsive
- [ ] Forms validate properly
- [ ] Error messages are user-friendly

### ✅ Security
- [ ] HTTPS enabled (automatic on Vercel)
- [ ] Environment variables not exposed in client
- [ ] Database connection secure
- [ ] File uploads validated
- [ ] Authentication working (if implemented)

### ✅ Performance
- [ ] Page load time < 3 seconds
- [ ] Images optimized
- [ ] No console errors
- [ ] Lighthouse score > 90

### ✅ Legal
- [ ] Privacy Policy page created
- [ ] Terms & Conditions page created
- [ ] Cookie consent banner (if required)
- [ ] GDPR compliance (if targeting EU)

### ✅ Monitoring
- [ ] Analytics enabled
- [ ] Error tracking configured
- [ ] Uptime monitoring active
- [ ] Backup strategy in place

---

## 🔧 Troubleshooting

### Build Fails on Vercel

**Error:** Module not found
```bash
# Solution: Clear Vercel cache
# Go to Vercel project → Settings → Git → Ignored Build Step
# Add: node_modules
```

**Error:** Environment variables not found
```bash
# Solution: Check variable names match exactly
# Redeploy after adding variables
```

### Database Connection Fails

**Error:** Connection refused
```bash
# Solution: Check DATABASE_URL format
# Should be: postgresql://user:password@host:port/database?schema=public
```

**Error:** SSL required
```bash
# Solution: Add ?sslmode=require to connection string
```

### Authentication Not Working

**Error:** Redirect URL mismatch
```bash
# Solution: Add your Vercel URL to Supabase
# Authentication → URL Configuration → Redirect URLs
```

---

## 📊 Cost Estimate (Free Tier)

### Supabase (Free)
- Database: 500 MB
- Storage: 1 GB
- Bandwidth: 2 GB/month
- Auth: 50,000 monthly active users

### Vercel (Hobby - Free)
- Bandwidth: 100 GB/month
- Serverless function executions: 100 GB-hours
- Build minutes: 6,000 minutes/month

### Total: **$0/month** for starting out

When you scale:
- Supabase Pro: $25/month
- Vercel Pro: $20/month
- **Total: ~$45/month** for serious traffic

---

## 🎉 You're Live!

Once deployed, your platform will be accessible at:
- **Vercel URL:** `https://thandizo-xxxxx.vercel.app`
- **Custom Domain:** `https://thandizo.com` (if configured)

### Share Your Platform

1. **Test thoroughly** before sharing
2. **Start with trusted users** (friends, family, partners)
3. **Gather feedback** and iterate
4. **Announce publicly** when ready

---

## 📞 Support Resources

- **Vercel Docs:** [vercel.com/docs](https://vercel.com/docs)
- **Supabase Docs:** [supabase.com/docs](https://supabase.com/docs)
- **Next.js Docs:** [nextjs.org/docs](https://nextjs.org/docs)
- **THANDIZO Docs:** See README.md and PHASE1_SUMMARY.md

---

## 🚀 Next Steps After Deployment

1. **Set up email notifications** for new applications
2. **Build admin dashboard** for reviewing applications
3. **Implement verification workflow**
4. **Create campaign pages**
5. **Integrate payment gateways**
6. **Add corporate partner portal**
7. **Launch marketing campaign**

---

**Congratulations! THANDIZO is now live and ready to help patients access medical care!** 🌿

# 🌿 THANDIZO — Phase 1 Foundation Complete

**THANDIZO is now running at http://localhost:3000**

This document summarizes what has been built in Phase 1 of the THANDIZO medical crowdfunding platform.

---

## ✅ What's Been Built

### 1. **Complete Database Schema** (`prisma/schema.prisma`)
A comprehensive PostgreSQL schema with 25+ entities covering:

- **Users & Authentication** — 9 user roles (Patient, Donor, Corporate Partner, Hospital Rep, Super Admin, Verification Officer, Financial Officer, Support Officer)
- **Patient Applications** — Multi-step application with medical info, documents, consent
- **Verification System** — Digital + physical verification with field reports
- **Campaigns** — Fundraising campaigns with updates and milestones
- **Donations & Financial** — Complete ledger system with 6 ledger types
- **Corporate Partners** — 4 partnership tiers (Community, Silver, Gold, Platinum)
- **Grants** — Grant tracking with allocation types
- **Hospitals** — Hospital registration and representative management
- **Notifications & Audit** — Full audit trail and notification system

**Key Features:**
- Application reference numbers (THD-2026-XXXXXX)
- Verification status workflow (11 statuses from DRAFT to FULLY_VERIFIED)
- Financial separation (patient funds vs platform revenue)
- Consent tracking with timestamps
- Role-based access control ready

### 2. **Design System** (`app/globals.css`, `lib/brand.ts`)
Complete THANDIZO brand identity:

- **Colors:** Forest Green #123E35, Emerald #21865D, Soft Mint #E8F4ED, Cream #FAFCFA
- **Typography:** Inter (sans) + Fraunces (display)
- **Components:** btn-primary, btn-secondary, btn-accent, card, input-field, badges
- **Utilities:** formatZMW(), generateReferenceNumber(), timeAgo()

### 3. **Landing Page** (`app/page.tsx`)
Professional, trust-building homepage with:

- **Hero Section** — Compelling headline with animated floating cards
- **Trust Bar** — 4 key trust indicators
- **How It Works** — 4-step visual process
- **Featured Patients** — Campaign cards with verification badges
- **Emergency Campaigns** — Urgent cases with countdown
- **Medical Categories** — 10 categories with icons
- **Verification Process** — Detailed 2-stage explanation
- **Corporate Partners** — 4 partnership tiers
- **Impact Stats** — Placeholder statistics (will show real data in production)
- **Testimonials** — 3 trust-building quotes
- **FAQ** — 6 common questions with expandable answers
- **Final CTA** — Strong call-to-action

**Design Highlights:**
- Fully responsive (mobile, tablet, desktop)
- Smooth animations (floating cards, hover effects)
- SVG illustrations (humanitarian healthcare theme)
- Clear verification badges
- Professional gradient backgrounds

### 4. **Patient Application Form** (`app/start/page.tsx`)
Complete 6-step multi-step form:

**Step 1: Personal Information**
- Full name, DOB, gender, nationality
- Address, city, province
- Phone, email

**Step 2: Medical Information**
- Medical condition, diagnosis
- Hospital details, attending doctor
- Procedure required, urgency level
- Estimated cost, additional notes

**Step 3: Documents**
- 6 document upload areas:
  - Medical reports (required)
  - Doctor's letter (required)
  - Hospital invoice (required)
  - Lab results (optional)
  - Prescriptions (optional)
  - Supporting documents (optional)

**Step 4: Identity & Representation**
- National ID (encrypted)
- Patient photograph
- Guardian/representative details

**Step 5: Fundraising**
- Requested amount, target date
- Campaign title, patient story
- Fundraising purpose
- Public campaign photo

**Step 6: Consent**
- 4 consent checkboxes:
  - Medical data processing
  - Identity verification
  - Campaign publication
  - Hospital contact
- Terms acceptance
- "What happens next" explanation

**Features:**
- Progress indicator with step icons
- Save as draft functionality
- Form validation
- Smooth transitions
- Mobile-optimized

### 5. **Navigation & Layout** (`components/layout.tsx`, `components/Header.tsx`)
- Sticky header with mobile menu
- Logo with heart icon and gold accent
- 4 main navigation items
- Sign In, Start Fundraiser, Donate Now CTAs
- Comprehensive footer with 4 columns
- Responsive design

### 6. **Demo Data** (`lib/demo-data.ts`)
Clearly labeled fictional data for development:

- 6 demo campaigns with different categories
- Impact milestones
- FAQ items
- Testimonials
- **Important:** All data is marked as demo/placeholder

## 🔧 Technical Stack

- **Framework:** Next.js 16 (App Router)
- **Language:** TypeScript
- **Styling:** Tailwind CSS 4
- **Database:** PostgreSQL (via Prisma ORM)
- **Authentication:** Supabase (or NextAuth.js)
- **File Storage:** Supabase Storage
- **Icons:** Lucide React
- **Fonts:** Inter + Fraunces (Google Fonts)

---

## 🎯 Key Design Decisions

### 1. **Two-Stage Verification**
Every patient must pass both digital and physical verification before receiving the "Fully Verified" badge. This builds trust and prevents fraud.

### 2. **Financial Separation**
Patient campaign funds are completely separate from platform operating revenue. This ensures transparency and accountability.

### 3. **Direct Hospital Payments**
Wherever possible, funds are paid directly to hospitals rather than individuals. This reduces fraud risk and ensures funds are used for medical treatment.

### 4. **Role-Based Access**
9 different user roles with granular permissions ensure that sensitive medical data is only accessible to authorized personnel.

### 5. **Audit Trail**
Every important action is logged with actor, timestamp, and metadata. This provides full accountability and compliance.

### 6. **Demo Data Labeling**
All placeholder data is clearly marked as demo/fictional. Real statistics will only appear once the platform receives actual applications and donations.

---

## 📊 Database Highlights

### Application Status Workflow
```
DRAFT → SUBMITTED → DIGITAL_REVIEW_PENDING → DIGITALLY_VERIFIED
→ AWAITING_PHYSICAL_VERIFICATION → PHYSICAL_VERIFICATION_COMPLETED
→ FULLY_VERIFIED (or REJECTED/SUSPENDED)
```

### Financial Ledger Types
1. PATIENT_CAMPAIGN_FUNDS
2. CORPORATE_SPONSORSHIP
3. GRANT_FUNDING
4. PLATFORM_OPERATING_INCOME
5. PLATFORM_EXPENSE
6. DISBURSEMENT
7. REFUND

### Partnership Tiers
- **Community Supporter:** K 10,000+
- **Silver Partner:** K 100,000+
- **Gold Partner:** K 500,000+
- **Platinum Partner:** K 2,000,000+

---

## 🔐 Security Features

- **Encrypted ID Storage:** National IDs are hashed, never stored in plaintext
- **Private Document Storage:** Medical documents stored in private Supabase buckets
- **Consent Tracking:** Explicit consent with timestamps for all data processing
- **Role-Based Access:** Granular permissions prevent unauthorized data access
- **Audit Logs:** Every admin action is logged
- **Secure Payments:** Server-side payment verification, no frontend-only confirmations

# 🌿 THANDIZO

**A Little Help Can Save a Life.**  
**Verified Care. Shared Hope.**

THANDIZO is a medical crowdfunding platform that connects people who cannot afford medical treatment with donors, companies, and communities willing to help.

The development server is already running at:
- **http://localhost:3000** — Landing page
- **http://localhost:3000/start** — Patient application form

```bash
cd /home/user/thandizo
npm install
```

```bash
npm run dev
```

### Build for Production
```bash
npm run build
npm start
```

# git ls-files --others --exclude-from=.git/info/exclude
# Lines that start with '#' are comments.
# For a project mostly in C, the following would be a good set of
# exclude patterns (uncomment them if you want to use them):
# *.[oa]
# *~

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to check the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with one argument, the name of the file
# that has the commit message.  The hook should exit with non-zero
# status after issuing an appropriate message if it wants to stop the
# commit.  The hook is allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "commit-msg".

# Uncomment the below to add a Signed-off-by line to the message.
# Doing this in a hook is a bad idea in general, but the prepare-commit-msg
# hook is more suited to it.
#
# SOB=$(git var GIT_AUTHOR_IDENT | sed -n 's/^\(.*>\).*$/Signed-off-by: \1/p')
# grep -qs "^$SOB" "$1" || echo "$SOB" >> "$1"

# This example catches duplicate Signed-off-by lines.

test "" = "$(grep '^Signed-off-by: ' "$1" |
	 sort | uniq -c | sed -e '/^[ 	]*1[ 	]/d')" || {
	echo >&2 Duplicate Signed-off-by lines.
	exit 1
}

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to prepare the commit log message.
# Called by "git commit" with the name of the file that has the
# commit message, followed by the description of the commit
# message's source.  The hook's purpose is to edit the commit
# message file.  If the hook fails with a non-zero status,
# the commit is aborted.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "prepare-commit-msg".

# This hook includes three examples. The first one removes the
# "# Please enter the commit message..." help message.
#
# The second includes the output of "git diff --name-status -r"
# into the message, just before the "git status" output.  It is
# commented because it doesn't cope with --amend or with squashed
# commits.
#
# The third example adds a Signed-off-by line to the message, that can
# still be edited.  This is rarely a good idea.

COMMIT_MSG_FILE=$1
COMMIT_SOURCE=$2
SHA1=$3

/usr/bin/perl -i.bak -ne 'print unless(m/^. Please enter the commit message/..m/^#$/)' "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"

#  *) ;;

# git interpret-trailers --in-place --trailer "$SOB" "$COMMIT_MSG_FILE"

#!/usr/bin/perl

use strict;
use warnings;
use IPC::Open2;

# An example hook script to integrate Watchman
# (https://facebook.github.io/watchman/) with git to speed up detecting
# new and modified files.
#
# The hook is passed a version (currently 2) and last update token
# formatted as a string and outputs to stdout a new update token and
# all files that have been modified since the update token. Paths must
# be relative to the root of the working tree and separated by a single NUL.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "query-watchman" and set
# 'git config core.fsmonitor .git/hooks/query-watchman'
#
my ($version, $last_update_token) = @ARGV;

# Uncomment for debugging
# print STDERR "$0 $version $last_update_token\n";

# Check the hook interface version
if ($version ne 2) {
	die "Unsupported query-fsmonitor hook version '$version'.\n" .
	    "Falling back to scanning...\n";
}

my $git_work_tree = get_working_dir();

my $retry = 1;

my $json_pkg;
eval {
	require JSON::XS;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::XS";
	1;
} or do {
	require JSON::PP;
	$json_pkg = "JSON::PP";
};

launch_watchman();

sub launch_watchman {
	my $o = watchman_query();
	if (is_work_tree_watched($o)) {
		output_result($o->{clock}, @{$o->{files}});
	}
}

sub output_result {
	my ($clockid, @files) = @_;

	# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
	# binmode $fh, ":utf8";
	# print $fh "$clockid\n@files\n";
	# close $fh;

	binmode STDOUT, ":utf8";
	print $clockid;
	print "\0";
	local $, = "\0";
	print @files;
}

sub watchman_clock {
	my $response = qx/watchman clock "$git_work_tree"/;
	die "Failed to get clock id on '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub watchman_query {
	my $pid = open2(\*CHLD_OUT, \*CHLD_IN, 'watchman -j --no-pretty')
	or die "open2() failed: $!\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n";

	# In the query expression below we're asking for names of files that
	# changed since $last_update_token but not from the .git folder.
	#
	# To accomplish this, we're using the "since" generator to use the
	# recency index to select candidate nodes and "fields" to limit the
	# output to file names only. Then we're using the "expression" term to
	# further constrain the results.
	my $last_update_line = "";
	if (substr($last_update_token, 0, 1) eq "c") {
		$last_update_token = "\"$last_update_token\"";
		$last_update_line = qq[\n"since": $last_update_token,];
	}
	my $query = <<"	END";
		["query", "$git_work_tree", {$last_update_line
			"fields": ["name"],
			"expression": ["not", ["dirname", ".git"]]
		}]
	END

	# Uncomment for debugging the watchman query
	# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-query.json");
	# print $fh $query;
	# close $fh;

	print CHLD_IN $query;
	close CHLD_IN;
	my $response = do {local $/; <CHLD_OUT>};

	# Uncomment for debugging the watch response
	# open ($fh, ">", ".git/watchman-response.json");
	# print $fh $response;
	# close $fh;

	die "Watchman: command returned no output.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $response eq "";
	die "Watchman: command returned invalid output: $response\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" unless $response =~ /^\{/;

	return $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
}

sub is_work_tree_watched {
	my ($output) = @_;
	my $error = $output->{error};
	if ($retry > 0 and $error and $error =~ m/unable to resolve root .* directory (.*) is not watched/) {
		$retry--;
		my $response = qx/watchman watch "$git_work_tree"/;
		die "Failed to make watchman watch '$git_work_tree'.\n" .
		    "Falling back to scanning...\n" if $? != 0;
		$output = $json_pkg->new->utf8->decode($response);
		$error = $output->{error};
		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		# Uncomment for debugging watchman output
		# open (my $fh, ">", ".git/watchman-output.out");
		# close $fh;

		# Watchman will always return all files on the first query so
		# return the fast "everything is dirty" flag to git and do the
		# Watchman query just to get it over with now so we won't pay
		# the cost in git to look up each individual file.
		my $o = watchman_clock();
		$error = $output->{error};

		die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
		"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

		output_result($o->{clock}, ("/"));
		$last_update_token = $o->{clock};

		eval { launch_watchman() };
		return 0;
	}

	die "Watchman: $error.\n" .
	"Falling back to scanning...\n" if $error;

	return 1;
}

sub get_working_dir {
	my $working_dir;
	if ($^O =~ 'msys' || $^O =~ 'cygwin') {
		$working_dir = Win32::GetCwd();
		$working_dir =~ tr/\\/\//;
	} else {
		require Cwd;
		$working_dir = Cwd::cwd();
	}

	return $working_dir;
}

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to block unannotated tags from entering.
# Called by "git receive-pack" with arguments: refname sha1-old sha1-new
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "update".
#
# Config
# ------
# hooks.allowunannotated
#   This boolean sets whether unannotated tags will be allowed into the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletetag
#   This boolean sets whether deleting tags will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.allowmodifytag
#   This boolean sets whether a tag may be modified after creation. By default
#   it won't be.
# hooks.allowdeletebranch
#   This boolean sets whether deleting branches will be allowed in the
#   repository.  By default they won't be.
# hooks.denycreatebranch
#   This boolean sets whether remotely creating branches will be denied
#   in the repository.  By default this is allowed.
#

# --- Command line
refname="$1"
oldrev="$2"
newrev="$3"

# --- Safety check
if [ -z "$GIT_DIR" ]; then
	echo "Don't run this script from the command line." >&2
	echo " (if you want, you could supply GIT_DIR then run" >&2
	echo "  $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>)" >&2
	exit 1
fi

if [ -z "$refname" -o -z "$oldrev" -o -z "$newrev" ]; then
	echo "usage: $0 <ref> <oldrev> <newrev>" >&2
	exit 1
fi

# --- Config
allowunannotated=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowunannotated)
allowdeletebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletebranch)
denycreatebranch=$(git config --type=bool hooks.denycreatebranch)
allowdeletetag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowdeletetag)
allowmodifytag=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allowmodifytag)

# check for no description
projectdesc=$(sed -e '1q' "$GIT_DIR/description")
case "$projectdesc" in
"Unnamed repository"* | "")
	echo "*** Project description file hasn't been set" >&2
	exit 1
	;;
esac

# --- Check types
# if $newrev is 0000...0000, it's a commit to delete a ref.
zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')
if [ "$newrev" = "$zero" ]; then
	newrev_type=delete
else
	newrev_type=$(git cat-file -t $newrev)
fi

case "$refname","$newrev_type" in
	refs/tags/*,commit)
		# un-annotated tag
		short_refname=${refname##refs/tags/}
		if [ "$allowunannotated" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** The un-annotated tag, $short_refname, is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			echo "*** Use 'git tag [ -a | -s ]' for tags you want to propagate." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,delete)
		# delete tag
		if [ "$allowdeletetag" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tag is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/tags/*,tag)
		# annotated tag
		if [ "$allowmodifytag" != "true" ] && git rev-parse $refname > /dev/null 2>&1
		then
			echo "*** Tag '$refname' already exists." >&2
			echo "*** Modifying a tag is not allowed in this repository." >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,commit)
		# branch
		if [ "$oldrev" = "$zero" -a "$denycreatebranch" = "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Creating a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/heads/*,delete)
		# delete branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,commit)
		# tracking branch
		;;
	refs/remotes/*,delete)
		# delete tracking branch
		if [ "$allowdeletebranch" != "true" ]; then
			echo "*** Deleting a tracking branch is not allowed in this repository" >&2
			exit 1
		fi
		;;
	*)
		# Anything else (is there anything else?)
		echo "*** Update hook: unknown type of update to ref $refname of type $newrev_type" >&2
		exit 1
		;;
esac

# --- Finished
exit 0

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed
# by applypatch from an e-mail message.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an
# appropriate message if it wants to stop the commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-applypatch".

. git-sh-setup
precommit="$(git rev-parse --git-path hooks/pre-commit)"
test -x "$precommit" && exec "$precommit" ${1+"$@"}
:

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed.
# Called by "git merge" with no arguments.  The hook should
# exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate message to
# stderr if it wants to stop the merge commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-merge-commit".

. git-sh-setup
test -x "$GIT_DIR/hooks/pre-commit" &&
        exec "$GIT_DIR/hooks/pre-commit"
:

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to verify what is about to be pushed.  Called by "git
# push" after it has checked the remote status, but before anything has been
# pushed.  If this script exits with a non-zero status nothing will be pushed.
#
# This hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- Name of the remote to which the push is being done
# $2 -- URL to which the push is being done
#
# If pushing without using a named remote those arguments will be equal.
#
# Information about the commits which are being pushed is supplied as lines to
# the standard input in the form:
#
#   <local ref> <local oid> <remote ref> <remote oid>
#
# This sample shows how to prevent push of commits where the log message starts
# with "WIP" (work in progress).

remote="$1"
url="$2"

zero=$(git hash-object --stdin </dev/null | tr '[0-9a-f]' '0')

while read local_ref local_oid remote_ref remote_oid
do
	if test "$local_oid" = "$zero"
	then
		# Handle delete
		:
	else
		if test "$remote_oid" = "$zero"
		then
			# New branch, examine all commits
			range="$local_oid"
		else
			# Update to existing branch, examine new commits
			range="$remote_oid..$local_oid"
		fi

		# Check for WIP commit
		commit=$(git rev-list -n 1 --grep '^WIP' "$range")
		if test -n "$commit"
		then
			echo >&2 "Found WIP commit in $local_ref, not pushing"
			exit 1
		fi
	fi
done

exit 0

exec git update-server-info

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to validate a patch (and/or patch series) before
# sending it via email.
#
# The hook should exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate
# message if it wants to prevent the email(s) from being sent.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "sendemail-validate".
#
# By default, it will only check that the patch(es) can be applied on top of
# the default upstream branch without conflicts in a secondary worktree. After
# validation (successful or not) of the last patch of a series, the worktree
# will be deleted.
#
# The following config variables can be set to change the default remote and
# remote ref that are used to apply the patches against:
#
#   sendemail.validateRemote (default: origin)
#   sendemail.validateRemoteRef (default: HEAD)
#
# Replace the TODO placeholders with appropriate checks according to your
# needs.

validate_cover_letter () {
	file="$1"
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks (e.g. spell checking).
	true
}

validate_patch () {
	file="$1"
	# Ensure that the patch applies without conflicts.
	git am -3 "$file" || return
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for this patch
	# (e.g. checkpatch.pl).
	true
}

validate_series () {
	# TODO: Replace with appropriate checks for the whole series
	# (e.g. quick build, coding style checks, etc.).
	true
}

# main -------------------------------------------------------------------------

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = 1
then
	remote=$(git config --default origin --get sendemail.validateRemote) &&
	ref=$(git config --default HEAD --get sendemail.validateRemoteRef) &&
	worktree=$(mktemp --tmpdir -d sendemail-validate.XXXXXXX) &&
	git worktree add -fd --checkout "$worktree" "refs/remotes/$remote/$ref" &&
	git config --replace-all sendemail.validateWorktree "$worktree"
else
	worktree=$(git config --get sendemail.validateWorktree)
fi || {
	echo "sendemail-validate: error: failed to prepare worktree" >&2
	exit 1
}

unset GIT_DIR GIT_WORK_TREE
cd "$worktree" &&

if grep -q "^diff --git " "$1"
then
	validate_patch "$1"
else
	validate_cover_letter "$1"
fi &&

if test "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_COUNTER" = "$GIT_SENDEMAIL_FILE_TOTAL"
then
	git config --unset-all sendemail.validateWorktree &&
	trap 'git worktree remove -ff "$worktree"' EXIT &&
	validate_series
fi

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to make use of push options.
# The example simply echoes all push options that start with 'echoback='
# and rejects all pushes when the "reject" push option is used.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-receive".

if test -n "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
then
	i=0
	while test "$i" -lt "$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_COUNT"
	do
		eval "value=\$GIT_PUSH_OPTION_$i"
		case "$value" in
		echoback=*)
			echo "echo from the pre-receive-hook: ${value#*=}" >&2
			;;
		reject)
			exit 1
		esac
		i=$((i + 1))
	done
fi

# appropriate message if it wants to stop the commit.  The hook is
# allowed to edit the commit message file.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "applypatch-msg".

. git-sh-setup
commitmsg="$(git rev-parse --git-path hooks/commit-msg)"
test -x "$commitmsg" && exec "$commitmsg" ${1+"$@"}
:

#!/bin/sh
#
# Copyright (c) 2006, 2008 Junio C Hamano
#
# The "pre-rebase" hook is run just before "git rebase" starts doing
# its job, and can prevent the command from running by exiting with
# non-zero status.
#
# The hook is called with the following parameters:
#
# $1 -- the upstream the series was forked from.
# $2 -- the branch being rebased (or empty when rebasing the current branch).
#
# This sample shows how to prevent topic branches that are already
# merged to 'next' branch from getting rebased, because allowing it
# would result in rebasing already published history.

publish=next
basebranch="$1"
if test "$#" = 2
then
	topic="refs/heads/$2"
else
	topic=`git symbolic-ref HEAD` ||
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt rebasing detached HEAD
fi

case "$topic" in
refs/heads/??/*)
	;;
*)
	exit 0 ;# we do not interrupt others.
	;;
esac

# Now we are dealing with a topic branch being rebased
# on top of master.  Is it OK to rebase it?

# Does the topic really exist?
git show-ref -q "$topic" || {
	echo >&2 "No such branch $topic"
	exit 1
}

# Is topic fully merged to master?
not_in_master=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^master "$topic"`
if test -z "$not_in_master"
then
	echo >&2 "$topic is fully merged to master; better remove it."
	exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
fi

# Is topic ever merged to next?  If so you should not be rebasing it.
only_next_1=`git rev-list ^master "^$topic" ${publish} | sort`
only_next_2=`git rev-list ^master           ${publish} | sort`
if test "$only_next_1" = "$only_next_2"
then
	not_in_topic=`git rev-list "^$topic" master`
	if test -z "$not_in_topic"
	then
		echo >&2 "$topic is already up to date with master"
		exit 1 ;# we could allow it, but there is no point.
	else
		exit 0
	fi
else
	not_in_next=`git rev-list --pretty=oneline ^${publish} "$topic"`
	/usr/bin/perl -e '
		my $topic = $ARGV[0];
		my $msg = "* $topic has commits already merged to public branch:\n";
		my (%not_in_next) = map {
			/^([0-9a-f]+) /;
			($1 => 1);
		} split(/\n/, $ARGV[1]);
		for my $elem (map {
				/^([0-9a-f]+) (.*)$/;
				[$1 => $2];
			} split(/\n/, $ARGV[2])) {
			if (!exists $not_in_next{$elem->[0]}) {
				if ($msg) {
					print STDERR $msg;
					undef $msg;
				}
				print STDERR " $elem->[1]\n";
			}
		}
	' "$topic" "$not_in_next" "$not_in_master"
	exit 1
fi

<<\DOC_END

This sample hook safeguards topic branches that have been
published from being rewound.

The workflow assumed here is:

 * Once a topic branch forks from "master", "master" is never
   merged into it again (either directly or indirectly).

 * Once a topic branch is fully cooked and merged into "master",
   it is deleted.  If you need to build on top of it to correct
   earlier mistakes, a new topic branch is created by forking at
   the tip of the "master".  This is not strictly necessary, but
   it makes it easier to keep your history simple.

 * Whenever you need to test or publish your changes to topic
   branches, merge them into "next" branch.

The script, being an example, hardcodes the publish branch name
to be "next", but it is trivial to make it configurable via
$GIT_DIR/config mechanism.

With this workflow, you would want to know:

(1) ... if a topic branch has ever been merged to "next".  Young
    topic branches can have stupid mistakes you would rather
    clean up before publishing, and things that have not been
    merged into other branches can be easily rebased without
    affecting other people.  But once it is published, you would
    not want to rewind it.

(2) ... if a topic branch has been fully merged to "master".
    Then you can delete it.  More importantly, you should not
    build on top of it -- other people may already want to
    change things related to the topic as patches against your
    "master", so if you need further changes, it is better to
    fork the topic (perhaps with the same name) afresh from the
    tip of "master".

Let's look at this example:

		   o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "next"
		  /       /           /           /
		 /   a---a---b A     /           /
		/   /               /           /
	       /   /   c---c---c---c B         /
	      /   /   /             \         /
	     /   /   /   b---b C     \       /
	    /   /   /   /             \     /
    ---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o---o "master"


A, B and C are topic branches.

 * A has one fix since it was merged up to "next".

 * B has finished.  It has been fully merged up to "master" and "next",
   and is ready to be deleted.

 * C has not merged to "next" at all.

We would want to allow C to be rebased, refuse A, and encourage
B to be deleted.

To compute (1):

	git rev-list ^master ^topic next
	git rev-list ^master        next

	if these match, topic has not merged in next at all.

To compute (2):

	git rev-list master..topic

	if this is empty, it is fully merged to "master".

DOC_END

#!/bin/sh
#
# An example hook script to verify what is about to be committed.
# Called by "git commit" with no arguments.  The hook should
# exit with non-zero status after issuing an appropriate message if
# it wants to stop the commit.
#
# To enable this hook, rename this file to "pre-commit".

if git rev-parse --verify HEAD >/dev/null 2>&1
then
	against=HEAD
else
	# Initial commit: diff against an empty tree object
	against=$(git hash-object -t tree /dev/null)
fi

# If you want to allow non-ASCII filenames set this variable to true.
allownonascii=$(git config --type=bool hooks.allownonascii)

# Redirect output to stderr.
exec 1>&2

# Cross platform projects tend to avoid non-ASCII filenames; prevent
# them from being added to the repository. We exploit the fact that the
# printable range starts at the space character and ends with tilde.
if [ "$allownonascii" != "true" ] &&
	# Note that the use of brackets around a tr range is ok here, (it's
	# even required, for portability to Solaris 10's /usr/bin/tr), since
	# the square bracket bytes happen to fall in the designated range.
	test $(git diff-index --cached --name-only --diff-filter=A -z $against |
	  LC_ALL=C tr -d '[ -~]\0' | wc -c) != 0
then
	cat <<\EOF
Error: Attempt to add a non-ASCII file name.

This can cause problems if you want to work with people on other platforms.

To be portable it is advisable to rename the file.

If you know what you are doing you can disable this check using:

  git config hooks.allownonascii true
EOF
	exit 1
fi

# If there are whitespace errors, print the offending file names and fail.
exec git diff-index --check --cached $against --

#!/bin/sh

# An example hook script to update a checked-out tree on a git push.
#
# This hook is invoked by git-receive-pack(1) when it reacts to git
# push and updates reference(s) in its repository, and when the push
# tries to update the branch that is currently checked out and the
# receive.denyCurrentBranch configuration variable is set to
# updateInstead.
#
# By default, such a push is refused if the working tree and the index
# of the remote repository has any difference from the currently
# checked out commit; when both the working tree and the index match
# the current commit, they are updated to match the newly pushed tip
# of the branch. This hook is to be used to override the default
# behaviour; however the code below reimplements the default behaviour
# as a starting point for convenient modification.
#
# The hook receives the commit with which the tip of the current
# branch is going to be updated:
commit=$1

# It can exit with a non-zero status to refuse the push (when it does
# so, it must not modify the index or the working tree).
die () {
	echo >&2 "$*"
	exit 1
}

# Or it can make any necessary changes to the working tree and to the
# index to bring them to the desired state when the tip of the current
# branch is updated to the new commit, and exit with a zero status.
#
# For example, the hook can simply run git read-tree -u -m HEAD "$1"
# in order to emulate git fetch that is run in the reverse direction
# with git push, as the two-tree form of git read-tree -u -m is
# essentially the same as git switch or git checkout that switches
# branches while keeping the local changes in the working tree that do
# not interfere with the difference between the branches.

# The below is a more-or-less exact translation to shell of the C code
# for the default behaviour for git's push-to-checkout hook defined in
# the push_to_deploy() function in builtin/receive-pack.c.
#
# Note that the hook will be executed from the repository directory,
# not from the working tree, so if you want to perform operations on
# the working tree, you will have to adapt your code accordingly, e.g.
# by adding "cd .." or using relative paths.

if ! git update-index -q --ignore-submodules --refresh
then
	die "Up-to-date check failed"
fi

if ! git diff-files --quiet --ignore-submodules --
then
	die "Working directory has unstaged changes"
fi

# This is a rough translation of:
#
#   head_has_history() ? "HEAD" : EMPTY_TREE_SHA1_HEX
if git cat-file -e HEAD 2>/dev/null
then
	head=HEAD
else
	head=$(git hash-object -t tree --stdin </dev/null)
fi

if ! git diff-index --quiet --cached --ignore-submodules $head --
then
	die "Working directory has staged changes"
fi

if ! git read-tree -u -m "$commit"
then
	die "Could not update working tree to new HEAD"
fi

[remote "origin"]
	url = https://github.com/teddymaposa1000/thandizo.git
	fetch = +refs/heads/*:refs/remotes/origin/*

@import "tailwindcss";

@theme {
  --color-forest: #123E35;
  --color-forest-dark: #0B2820;
  --color-forest-light: #1A5548;
  --color-emerald-th: #21865D;
  --color-emerald-light: #2BA374;
  --color-mint: #E8F4ED;
  --color-mint-dark: #D4EBDD;
  --color-cream: #FAFCFA;
  --color-ink: #26332D;
  --color-ink-light: #4A5B52;
  --color-sage: #F2F5F3;
  --color-gold: #C9A961;
  --color-coral: #E07856;
  --color-sky: #5B9BD5;

  --font-sans: 'Inter', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --font-display: 'Fraunces', Georgia, serif;
}

@layer base {
  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
  }

  body {
    background: var(--color-cream);
    color: var(--color-ink);
    font-family: var(--font-sans);
  }

  h1, h2, h3, h4 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
  }
}

@layer components {
  .btn-primary {
    @apply inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-forest text-white font-medium rounded-full hover:bg-forest-light transition-all duration-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed;
  }

  .btn-secondary {
    @apply inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-white text-forest border-2 border-forest font-medium rounded-full hover:bg-mint transition-all duration-200 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed;
  }

  .btn-accent {
    @apply inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-emerald-th text-white font-medium rounded-full hover:bg-emerald-light transition-all duration-200 shadow-sm;
  }

  .card {
    @apply bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow duration-200;
  }

  .input-field {
    @apply w-full px-4 py-3 bg-white border border-sage rounded-xl text-ink placeholder:text-ink-light/50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-th/30 focus:border-emerald-th transition-all;
  }

  .label {
    @apply block text-sm font-medium text-ink mb-1.5;
  }

  .badge-verified {
    @apply inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-mint text-forest text-xs font-medium rounded-full border border-emerald-th/20;
  }

  .badge-pending {
    @apply inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 text-xs font-medium rounded-full border border-amber-200;
  }

  .badge-urgent {
    @apply inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-red-50 text-red-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-full border border-red-200;
  }
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Inter, Fraunces } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";

const inter = Inter({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-inter",
  display: "swap",
});

const fraunces = Fraunces({
  subsets: ["latin"],
  variable: "--font-fraunces",
  display: "swap",
  weight: ["400", "500", "600", "700"],
});

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Thandizo — Verified Care. Shared Hope.",
  description:
    "Thandizo connects people who cannot afford medical treatment with donors, companies and communities willing to help. A little help can save a life.",
  keywords: [
    "medical crowdfunding",
    "Zambia healthcare",
    "medical fundraising",
    "patient assistance",
    "healthcare donations",
  ],
  openGraph: {
    title: "Thandizo — Verified Care. Shared Hope.",
    description:
      "A humanitarian technology platform connecting patients in need with those who can help.",
    type: "website",
  },
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: Readonly<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" className={`${inter.variable} ${fraunces.variable}`}>
      <body className="antialiased">
        {children}
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import {
  Heart,
  Shield,
  Search,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  Building2,
  Stethoscope,
  Users,
  HandCoins,
  BadgeCheck,
  Eye,
  Handshake,
  ChevronRight,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  Plus,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Header, Footer } from "@/components/layout";
import { BRAND, MEDICAL_CATEGORIES, formatZMW } from "@/lib/brand";

  DEMO_CAMPAIGNS,
  IMPACT_MILESTONES,
  FAQ_ITEMS,
  TESTIMONIALS,
} from "@/lib/demo-data";

export default function HomePage() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
      <Header />

      <main className="flex-1">
        <HeroSection />
        <TrustBar />
        <HowItWorks />
        <FeaturedPatients />
        <EmergencyCampaigns />
        <MedicalCategories />
        <VerificationProcess />
        <CorporatePartners />
        <ImpactStats />
        <Testimonials />
        <FAQSection />
        <FinalCTA />
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

function HeroSection() {
  return (
    <section className="relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-b from-mint via-cream to-cream">
      {/* Decorative gradient blobs */}
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-[600px] h-[600px] bg-emerald-th/10 rounded-full blur-3xl -translate-y-1/2 translate-x-1/3 pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-[500px] h-[500px] bg-forest/5 rounded-full blur-3xl translate-y-1/2 -translate-x-1/3 pointer-events-none" />

      <div className="relative max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 pt-16 pb-24 lg:pt-24 lg:pb-32">
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-12 lg:gap-16 items-center">
          {/* Left: Copy */}
          <div>
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white rounded-full border border-emerald-th/20 text-xs font-medium text-forest mb-6 shadow-sm">
              <BadgeCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-th" />
              Every patient is independently verified
            </div>

            <h1 className="font-display text-4xl sm:text-5xl lg:text-6xl font-semibold text-forest leading-[1.05] tracking-tight">
              Healthcare Should{" "}
              <span className="relative inline-block">
                <span className="relative z-10">Never Be</span>
                <span className="absolute bottom-2 left-0 right-0 h-3 bg-gold/30 -z-0 rounded" />
              </span>{" "}
              Out of Reach.
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-6 text-lg text-ink-light leading-relaxed max-w-xl">
              Thousands of people struggle to access essential medical treatment
              because of financial challenges. Thandizo connects people in need with
              those who can help — transparently, accountably, and with verified
              impact.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3">
              <Link href="/patients" className="btn-primary !px-8 !py-4 text-base">
                <Search className="w-5 h-5" />
                Find Someone to Help
              </Link>
              <Link href="/start" className="btn-secondary !px-8 !py-4 text-base">
                Get Medical Assistance
                <ArrowRight className="w-5 h-5" />
              </Link>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-10 flex items-center gap-6 text-sm text-ink-light">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th" />
                <span>Free to apply</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th" />
                <span>Direct hospital payments</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Right: Visual */}
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="relative aspect-[4/5] max-w-md mx-auto">
              {/* Main image card */}
              <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-th to-forest">
                {/* Placeholder humanitarian healthcare visual using SVG */}
                <svg viewBox="0 0 400 500" className="w-full h-full" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
                  <defs>
                    <linearGradient id="heroGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
                      <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#21865D" />
                      <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#123E35" />
                    </linearGradient>
                    <linearGradient id="sunGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                      <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#C9A961" stopOpacity="0.8" />
                      <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#C9A961" stopOpacity="0" />
                    </linearGradient>
                  </defs>
                  <rect width="400" height="500" fill="url(#heroGrad)" />
                  <circle cx="320" cy="80" r="60" fill="url(#sunGrad)" />

                  {/* Stylized figures — healthcare worker and patient */}
                  <g opacity="0.95">
                    {/* Healthcare worker */}
                    <circle cx="150" cy="240" r="28" fill="#FAFCFA" />
                    <path d="M110 340 Q110 290 150 290 Q190 290 190 340 L190 420 L110 420 Z" fill="#FAFCFA" />
                    {/* Cross on uniform */}
                    <rect x="145" y="320" width="10" height="30" fill="#21865D" rx="2" />
                    <rect x="140" y="330" width="20" height="10" fill="#21865D" rx="2" />

                    {/* Patient */}
                    <circle cx="260" cy="250" r="24" fill="#E8F4ED" />
                    <path d="M225 345 Q225 305 260 305 Q295 305 295 345 L295 420 L225 420 Z" fill="#E8F4ED" />
                  </g>

                  {/* Hands connecting */}
                  <g opacity="0.9">
                    <path d="M185 360 Q205 345 225 360" stroke="#C9A961" strokeWidth="4" fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />
                  </g>

                  {/* Heart in middle */}
                  <g transform="translate(205 330)">
                    <path d="M0 5 C-8 -8, -20 0, -10 10 L0 22 L10 10 C20 0, 8 -8, 0 5 Z" fill="#C9A961" />
                  </g>

                  {/* Decorative dots */}
                  <circle cx="50" cy="120" r="3" fill="#E8F4ED" opacity="0.6" />
                  <circle cx="80" cy="160" r="2" fill="#E8F4ED" opacity="0.4" />
                  <circle cx="340" cy="200" r="2.5" fill="#E8F4ED" opacity="0.5" />
                  <circle cx="360" cy="260" r="2" fill="#E8F4ED" opacity="0.4" />
                </svg>
              </div>

              {/* Floating card — verified badge */}
              <div className="absolute -left-6 top-12 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-4 w-48 animate-float">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-mint flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Shield className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-th" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-forest">Fully Verified</div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-ink-light">Physical visit complete</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Floating card — donation */}
              <div className="absolute -right-4 bottom-16 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-4 w-52 animate-float-delayed">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span className="text-xs text-ink-light">New donation</span>
                  <Heart className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-coral" fill="currentColor" />
                </div>
                <div className="text-lg font-semibold text-forest">K 2,500</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-ink-light mt-1">from a donor in Lusaka</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

    </section>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// TRUST BAR
// ============================================================
function TrustBar() {
  const items = [
    { icon: Shield, text: "Two-stage verification" },
    { icon: Stethoscope, text: "Hospital-confirmed cases" },
    { icon: Building2, text: "Direct hospital payments" },
    { icon: Eye, text: "Transparent disbursements" },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="bg-white border-y border-sage">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
          {items.map(({ icon: Icon, text }) => (
            <div key={text} className="flex items-center gap-3 justify-center lg:justify-start">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-mint flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-forest" />
              </div>
              <span className="text-sm font-medium text-ink">{text}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// HOW IT WORKS
// ============================================================
function HowItWorks() {
  const steps = [
    {
      step: "01",
      title: "Apply",
      description:
        "Patients or their representatives submit a detailed medical assistance application with supporting documentation.",
      icon: Plus,
    },
    {
      step: "02",
      title: "Verify",
      description:
        "Our team conducts digital verification, followed by an independent physical visit to confirm the case in person.",
      icon: Shield,
    },
    {
      step: "03",
      title: "Fundraise",
      description:
        "Approved campaigns go live to donors, corporate partners and foundations who contribute toward the target.",
      icon: HandCoins,
    },
    {
      step: "04",
      title: "Deliver",
      description:
        "Funds are paid directly to the hospital where possible, with full audit trail and proof of payment.",
      icon: Handshake,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <section className="py-24 bg-cream">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <SectionHeader
          eyebrow="How Thandizo Works"
          title="A transparent path from need to care"
          description="Four carefully designed stages ensure every contribution reaches a verified patient and every patient receives accountable care."
        />

        <div className="mt-16 grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
          {steps.map((step, idx) => (
            <div key={step.step} className="relative">
              <div className="card p-6 h-full">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-th to-forest flex items-center justify-center">
                    <step.icon className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
                  </div>
                  <span className="font-display text-4xl font-semibold text-sage">
                    {step.step}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h3 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-forest mb-2">
                  {step.title}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-ink-light leading-relaxed">
                  {step.description}
                </p>
              </div>
              {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
                <div className="hidden lg:block absolute top-1/2 -right-3 -translate-y-1/2 z-10">
                  <ChevronRight className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-th/40" />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function FeaturedPatients() {

  return (

            title="Cases ready for your support"
            align="left"
            className="mb-0"
          />
          <Link
            href="/patients"
            className="btn-secondary !py-3 self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            View all patients
            <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </Link>
        </div>

        <DemoNotice />

        <div className="mt-6 grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {featured.map((c) => (
            <CampaignCard key={c.id} campaign={c} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

}

// ============================================================
// EMERGENCY CAMPAIGNS
// ============================================================
function EmergencyCampaigns() {
  const emergencies = DEMO_CAMPAIGNS.filter((c) => c.urgency === "EMERGENCY");

  return (
    <section className="py-24 bg-gradient-to-br from-forest to-forest-dark text-white relative overflow-hidden">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-10">
        <svg width="100%" height="100%" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
          <pattern id="grid" width="40" height="40" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <path d="M 40 0 L 0 0 0 40" fill="none" stroke="white" strokeWidth="0.5" />
          </pattern>
          <rect width="100%" height="100%" fill="url(#grid)" />
        </svg>
      </div>

      <div className="relative max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-coral/20 backdrop-blur flex items-center justify-center">
            <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-coral" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-medium text-coral uppercase tracking-widest">
            Urgent medical needs
          </span>
        </div>

        <h2 className="font-display text-4xl sm:text-5xl font-semibold mb-4 max-w-3xl">
          Emergency campaigns that can't wait.
        </h2>
        <p className="text-white/70 text-lg mb-10 max-w-2xl">
          These verified patients have time-critical medical needs. Every hour counts.
        </p>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          {emergencies.map((c) => (
            <div key={c.id} className="bg-white/5 backdrop-blur border border-white/10 rounded-2xl p-6 hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                <div className={`w-16 h-16 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br ${c.gradient} flex items-center justify-center text-white text-2xl font-semibold flex-shrink-0`}>
                  {c.patientInitial}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 flex-wrap">
                    <span className="badge-urgent">
                      <Clock className="w-3 h-3" /> {c.daysLeft} days left
                    </span>
                    {c.verificationLevel === "FULL" && (
                      <span className="badge-verified !bg-white/10 !text-white !border-white/20">
                        <Shield className="w-3 h-3" /> Fully Verified
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold mb-1 truncate">
                    {c.title}
                  </h3>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-white/60 mb-4">
                    <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    {c.location}
                  </div>

                  <CampaignProgress
                    raised={c.raisedAmount}
                    target={c.targetAmount}
                    variant="light"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// MEDICAL CATEGORIES
// ============================================================
function MedicalCategories() {
  return (
    <section className="py-24 bg-cream">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <SectionHeader
          eyebrow="Medical Categories"
          title="Find a cause that speaks to you"
          description="Support the kind of medical need that matters most to you — from emergency surgeries to long-term rehabilitation."
        />

        <div className="mt-14 grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
          {MEDICAL_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
            <Link
              key={cat.id}
              href={`/patients?category=${cat.id}`}
              className="card p-5 hover:border-emerald-th/30 hover:-translate-y-0.5 transition-all group"
            >
              <div className="text-3xl mb-3">{cat.icon}</div>
              <div className="font-medium text-forest text-sm leading-tight group-hover:text-emerald-th transition-colors">
                {cat.label}
              </div>
              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-ink-light/50 mt-3 group-hover:text-emerald-th group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-all" />
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// VERIFICATION PROCESS
// ============================================================
function VerificationProcess() {
  return (
    <section className="py-24 bg-mint/40">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-16 items-center">
          <div>
            <span className="inline-block text-xs font-medium text-emerald-th uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">
              Our Verification Process
            </span>
            <h2 className="font-display text-4xl sm:text-5xl font-semibold text-forest leading-tight mb-6">
              Trust is earned,<br />not assumed.
            </h2>
            <p className="text-lg text-ink-light leading-relaxed mb-8">
              Thandizo operates a rigorous two-stage verification process. Every
              patient must be confirmed through documentation and in-person before
              they can receive the highest verification badge.
            </p>

            <div className="space-y-4">
              <VerificationStage
                number="1"
                title="Digital Verification"
                items={[
                  "Identity documentation review",
                  "Medical report authentication",
                  "Hospital and doctor confirmation",
                  "Treatment quotation review",
                  "Consent verification",
                ]}
              />
              <VerificationStage
                number="2"
                title="Physical Verification"
                items={[
                  "Independent officer visit",
                  "Patient and guardian interview",
                  "Hospital personnel contact",
                  "Evidence collection with consent",
                  "Field report and recommendation",
                ]}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Visual */}
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="aspect-square max-w-md mx-auto relative">
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-th to-forest rounded-3xl rotate-3 opacity-20" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl p-8 flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-6">
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-ink-light uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Verification Status
                    </span>
                    <span className="badge-verified">
                      <Shield className="w-3 h-3" />
                      Fully Verified
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest mb-2">
                    Sample Patient Record
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-sm text-ink-light">
                    Visual representation of what a verified patient record looks like to donors.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {[
                    { label: "Identity verified", done: true },
                    { label: "Medical report authenticated", done: true },
                    { label: "Hospital confirmation", done: true },
                    { label: "Physical visit completed", done: true },
                    { label: "Field report approved", done: true },
                  ].map((item) => (
                    <div key={item.label} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-emerald-th/10 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th" />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-sm text-ink">{item.label}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function VerificationStage({ number, title, items }: { number: string; title: string; items: string[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-forest text-white font-display font-semibold flex items-center justify-center">
          {number}
        </div>
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest">{title}</h3>
      </div>
      <ul className="space-y-2 pl-1">
        {items.map((item) => (
          <li key={item} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-ink-light">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
            {item}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

function CorporatePartners() {
  const tiers = [
    { tier: "Community", color: "from-ink-light to-ink", min: "K 10,000+" },
    { tier: "Silver", color: "from-slate-400 to-slate-600", min: "K 100,000+" },

  return (
    <section className="py-24 bg-cream">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <SectionHeader
          eyebrow="Corporate Partnerships"
          title="CSR with measurable impact"

        />

        <div className="mt-14 grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          {tiers.map((t) => (
            <div key={t.tier} className="card overflow-hidden group hover:-translate-y-1 transition-transform">
              <div className={`h-24 bg-gradient-to-br ${t.color} flex items-end p-4`}>

              </div>

                  {t.min}
                </div>
                <ul className="space-y-2 text-sm text-ink-light">
                  <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-th" />
                    Impact reporting
                  </li>
                  <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-th" />
                    Sponsorship statements
                  </li>
                  <li className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-th" />

        </div>
      </div>

}
