// ============================================================
// SHARED COMPONENTS
// ============================================================
function SectionHeader({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  align = "center",
  className = "",
}: {
  eyebrow?: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  align?: "center" | "left";
  className?: string;
}) {
  const alignClass = align === "center" ? "text-center mx-auto" : "text-left";
  return (
    <div className={`max-w-3xl ${alignClass} ${className}`}>
      {eyebrow && (
        <span className="inline-block text-xs font-medium text-emerald-th uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">
          {eyebrow}
        </span>
      )}
      <h2 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl font-semibold text-forest leading-tight">
        {title}
      </h2>
      {description && (
        <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-ink-light leading-relaxed">{description}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function DemoNotice() {
  return (
    <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs text-amber-900 flex items-start gap-2">
      <span className="font-semibold">Development build:</span>
      <span>
        Patient stories and figures shown are illustrative placeholders. Real
        verified cases will appear when the platform launches.
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function CampaignCard({ campaign }: { campaign: any }) {
  const progress = (campaign.raisedAmount / campaign.targetAmount) * 100;

  return (
    <Link href={`/patients/${campaign.slug}`} className="card overflow-hidden group block">
      <div className={`aspect-[4/3] bg-gradient-to-br ${campaign.gradient} relative`}>
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
          <span className="text-white/90 font-display text-6xl font-semibold">
            {campaign.patientInitial}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 flex gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
          {campaign.verificationLevel === "FULL" && (
            <span className="badge-verified">
              <Shield className="w-3 h-3" /> Verified
            </span>
          )}
          {campaign.urgency === "EMERGENCY" && (
            <span className="badge-urgent">
              <Clock className="w-3 h-3" /> Urgent
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-forest flex items-center gap-1">
          <MapPin className="w-3 h-3" />
          {campaign.location}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-5">
        <div className="text-xs font-medium text-emerald-th uppercase tracking-wider mb-1.5">
          {MEDICAL_CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === campaign.category)?.label}
        </div>
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest mb-2 group-hover:text-emerald-th transition-colors line-clamp-2">
          {campaign.title}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-light line-clamp-2 mb-4">
          {campaign.story}
        </p>

        <CampaignProgress
          raised={campaign.raisedAmount}
          target={campaign.targetAmount}
          donorCount={campaign.donorCount}
        />
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

function CampaignProgress({
  raised,
  target,
  donorCount,
  variant = "default",
}: {
  raised: number;
  target: number;
  donorCount?: number;
  variant?: "default" | "light";
}) {
  const progress = Math.min((raised / target) * 100, 100);
  const isLight = variant === "light";

  return (
    <div>
      <div className={`h-1.5 rounded-full ${isLight ? "bg-white/20" : "bg-sage"} overflow-hidden mb-2`}>
        <div
          className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-th to-forest rounded-full transition-all"
          style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
        />
      </div>
      <div className={`flex items-center justify-between text-xs ${isLight ? "text-white/80" : "text-ink-light"}`}>
        <span>
          <span className={`font-semibold ${isLight ? "text-white" : "text-forest"}`}>
            {formatZMW(raised)}
          </span>{" "}
          of {formatZMW(target)}
        </span>
        {donorCount !== undefined && (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
            <Users className="w-3 h-3" />
            {donorCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  User,
  Stethoscope,
  FileText,
  Shield,
  Heart,
  CheckCircle2,
  ArrowRight,
  ArrowLeft,
  Upload,
  AlertCircle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Header, Footer } from "@/components/layout";

type Step = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6;

export default function StartFundraiserPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState<Step>(1);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    // Step 1: Personal
    fullName: "",
    dateOfBirth: "",
    gender: "",
    nationality: "Zambian",
    address: "",
    city: "",
    province: "",
    phone: "",
    email: "",

    // Step 2: Medical
    medicalCondition: "",
    diagnosis: "",
    situationDescription: "",
    hospitalName: "",
    hospitalLocation: "",
    attendingDoctor: "",
    procedureRequired: "",
    urgency: "MEDIUM",
    estimatedCost: "",
    additionalNotes: "",

    // Step 3: Documents (handled separately)
    documents: [] as File[],

    // Step 4: Identity
    nationalId: "",
    patientPhoto: null as File | null,
    guardianName: "",
    guardianRelationship: "",
    guardianPhone: "",

    // Step 5: Fundraising
    requestedAmount: "",
    campaignTitle: "",
    patientStory: "",
    fundraisingPurpose: "",
    targetDate: "",

    // Step 6: Consent
    medicalDataConsent: false,
    identityConsent: false,
    publicationConsent: false,
    hospitalContactConsent: false,
  });

  const steps = [
    { num: 1, title: "Personal Info", icon: User },
    { num: 2, title: "Medical Details", icon: Stethoscope },
    { num: 3, title: "Documents", icon: FileText },
    { num: 4, title: "Identity", icon: Shield },
    { num: 5, title: "Fundraising", icon: Heart },
    { num: 6, title: "Consent", icon: CheckCircle2 },
  ];

  const updateField = (field: string, value: any) => {
    setFormData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
  };

  const nextStep = () => {
    if (currentStep < 6) setCurrentStep((currentStep + 1) as Step);
  };

  const prevStep = () => {
    if (currentStep > 1) setCurrentStep((currentStep - 1) as Step);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async () => {
    // In production, this would submit to the API
    alert("Application submitted! (Demo mode — no data saved)");
    router.push("/dashboard/patient");
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col">
      <Header />

      <main className="flex-1 py-12 bg-sage/30">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="mb-8">
            <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-semibold text-forest mb-2">
              Start a Medical Fundraiser
            </h1>
            <p className="text-ink-light">
              Complete this application to request medical assistance. You can save
              your progress and return later.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Progress Steps */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage p-6 mb-8">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              {steps.map((step, idx) => {
                const Icon = step.icon;
                const isActive = currentStep === step.num;
                const isCompleted = currentStep > step.num;

                return (
                  <div key={step.num} className="flex items-center flex-1">
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                      <div
                        className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
                          isActive
                            ? "bg-forest text-white shadow-lg scale-110"
                            : isCompleted
                            ? "bg-emerald-th text-white"
                            : "bg-sage text-ink-light"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isCompleted ? (
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6" />
                        ) : (
                          <Icon className="w-5 h-5" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <span
                        className={`mt-2 text-xs font-medium text-center ${
                          isActive ? "text-forest" : "text-ink-light"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {step.title}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
                      <div
                        className={`flex-1 h-0.5 mx-2 ${
                          isCompleted ? "bg-emerald-th" : "bg-sage"
                        }`}
                      />
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Form */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-sage p-8">
            {currentStep === 1 && <Step1Personal formData={formData} updateField={updateField} />}
            {currentStep === 2 && <Step2Medical formData={formData} updateField={updateField} />}
            {currentStep === 3 && <Step3Documents formData={formData} updateField={updateField} />}
            {currentStep === 4 && <Step4Identity formData={formData} updateField={updateField} />}
            {currentStep === 5 && <Step5Fundraising formData={formData} updateField={updateField} />}
            {currentStep === 6 && <Step6Consent formData={formData} updateField={updateField} />}

            {/* Navigation */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-sage">
              <button
                onClick={prevStep}
                disabled={currentStep === 1}
                className="btn-secondary disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
                Previous
              </button>

              {currentStep < 6 ? (
                <button onClick={nextStep} className="btn-primary">
                  Next Step
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button onClick={handleSubmit} className="btn-primary">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  Submit Application
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Save Progress */}
          <div className="mt-6 text-center">
            <button className="text-sm text-ink-light hover:text-forest transition-colors">
              Save as draft and continue later
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      <Footer />
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// STEP COMPONENTS
// ============================================================

function Step1Personal({ formData, updateField }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest mb-1">
          Personal Information
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-light">
          Tell us about the patient seeking medical assistance.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="label">Full Legal Name *</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={formData.fullName}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("fullName", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
            placeholder="As shown on ID"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="label">Date of Birth *</label>
          <input
            type="date"
            value={formData.dateOfBirth}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("dateOfBirth", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="label">Gender</label>
          <select
            value={formData.gender}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("gender", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          >
            <option value="">Select</option>
            <option value="male">Male</option>
            <option value="female">Female</option>
            <option value="other">Other</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="label">Nationality</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={formData.nationality}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("nationality", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="label">Residential Address</label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={formData.address}
          onChange={(e) => updateField("address", e.target.value)}
          className="input-field"
          placeholder="Street address"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="label">City</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={formData.city}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("city", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="label">Province</label>
          <select
            value={formData.province}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("province", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          >
            <option value="">Select province</option>
            <option value="lusaka">Lusaka</option>
            <option value="copperbelt">Copperbelt</option>
            <option value="southern">Southern</option>
            <option value="eastern">Eastern</option>
            <option value="northern">Northern</option>
            <option value="western">Western</option>
            <option value="central">Central</option>
            <option value="luapula">Luapula</option>
            <option value="northwestern">North-Western</option>
            <option value="muchinga">Muchinga</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="label">Phone Number *</label>
          <input
            type="tel"
            value={formData.phone}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("phone", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
            placeholder="+260 97 1234567"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="label">Email Address *</label>
          <input
            type="email"
            value={formData.email}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("email", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
            placeholder="patient@example.com"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Step2Medical({ formData, updateField }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">

        </p>

      <div>

          type="text"

          onChange={(e) => updateField("medicalCondition", e.target.value)}

          placeholder="e.g., Heart disease, Cancer, Injury"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="label">Diagnosis *</label>
        <textarea
          value={formData.diagnosis}
          onChange={(e) => updateField("diagnosis", e.target.value)}
          className="input-field"
          rows={3}
          placeholder="Official diagnosis from doctor"
        />
      </div>

      <div>

          value={formData.situationDescription}
          onChange={(e) => updateField("situationDescription", e.target.value)}
          className="input-field"
          rows={4}
          placeholder="Describe the patient's medical situation and why assistance is needed"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="label">Hospital / Clinic Name *</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={formData.hospitalName}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("hospitalName", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="label">Hospital Location</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={formData.hospitalLocation}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("hospitalLocation", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
            placeholder="City, Province"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="label">Attending Doctor</label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={formData.attendingDoctor}
          onChange={(e) => updateField("attendingDoctor", e.target.value)}
          className="input-field"
          placeholder="Dr. Name"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="label">Required Procedure *</label>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={formData.procedureRequired}
          onChange={(e) => updateField("procedureRequired", e.target.value)}
          className="input-field"
          placeholder="e.g., Heart surgery, Chemotherapy"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        <div>
          <label className="label">Treatment Urgency *</label>
          <select
            value={formData.urgency}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("urgency", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
          >
            <option value="LOW">Low — Can wait months</option>
            <option value="MEDIUM">Medium — Within weeks</option>
            <option value="HIGH">High — Within days</option>
            <option value="EMERGENCY">Emergency — Immediate</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label className="label">Estimated Cost (ZMW) *</label>
          <input
            type="number"
            value={formData.estimatedCost}
            onChange={(e) => updateField("estimatedCost", e.target.value)}
            className="input-field"
            placeholder="50000"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <label className="label">Additional Notes</label>
        <textarea
          value={formData.additionalNotes}
          onChange={(e) => updateField("additionalNotes", e.target.value)}
          className="input-field"
          rows={3}
          placeholder="Any other relevant medical information"
        />

    </div>
  );
}

function Step3Documents({ formData, updateField }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest mb-1">
          Medical Documentation
        </h2>

          Upload supporting medical documents. All files are stored securely and only
          accessible to authorized verification officers.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-mint/50 border border-emerald-th/20 rounded-xl p-4 flex gap-3">
        <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-th flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="text-sm text-ink-light">
          <strong className="text-forest">Accepted formats:</strong> PDF, JPG, PNG.
          Maximum 10MB per file.

      <div className="space-y-4">

function DocumentUpload({ label, description, required }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-sage rounded-xl p-6 hover:border-emerald-th/50 transition-colors">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-mint flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
          <Upload className="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-th" />
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <span className="font-medium text-forest">{label}</span>
            {required && <span className="text-coral text-xs">*</span>}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-light mb-3">{description}</p>
          <button className="btn-secondary !py-2 !px-4 text-sm">
            <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
            Choose Files
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Step4Identity({ formData, updateField }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest mb-1">

        label="Identity Document"

                type="text"

            <div>
              <label className="label">Guardian's Phone</label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                value={formData.guardianPhone}
                onChange={(e) => updateField("guardianPhone", e.target.value)}
                className="input-field"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Step5Fundraising({ formData, updateField }: any) {

    <div className="space-y-6">

          placeholder="Share the patient's story in a way that helps donors understand the situation and feel connected..."
        />

        </p>

          value={formData.fundraisingPurpose}

          rows={3}
          placeholder="How will the funds be used?"
        />
      </div>

      <DocumentUpload
        label="Public Campaign Photo"
        description="A photo to represent the campaign (with patient consent)"

}

function Step6Consent({ formData, updateField }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-semibold text-forest mb-1">
          Consent & Submission
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-light">
          Please review and accept the following consents before submitting.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        <ConsentCheckbox
          checked={formData.medicalDataConsent}
          onChange={(v: boolean) => updateField("medicalDataConsent", v)}
          label="Medical Data Processing"
          description="I consent to Thandizo processing the medical information provided for the purpose of verification and campaign management."
        />
        <ConsentCheckbox
          checked={formData.identityConsent}
          onChange={(v: boolean) => updateField("identityConsent", v)}
          label="Identity Verification"
          description="I consent to identity verification procedures, including secure storage of encrypted identity documents."
        />
        <ConsentCheckbox
          checked={formData.publicationConsent}
          onChange={(v: boolean) => updateField("publicationConsent", v)}
          label="Campaign Publication"
          description="I consent to the publication of the patient's story and approved photographs for fundraising purposes."
        />
        <ConsentCheckbox
          checked={formData.hospitalContactConsent}
          onChange={(v: boolean) => updateField("hospitalContactConsent", v)}
          label="Hospital Contact"
          description="I consent to Thandizo contacting the hospital and medical professionals to verify treatment details."
        />
      </div>

      <div className="bg-mint/50 border border-emerald-th/20 rounded-xl p-5">
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest mb-3">
          Before You Submit
        </h3>
        <ul className="space-y-2 text-sm text-ink-light">
          <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
            All information provided is accurate to the best of my knowledge
          </li>
          <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
            I understand that false information may result in application rejection
          </li>
          <li className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-th mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0" />
            I agree to Thandizo's Terms & Conditions and Privacy Policy
          </li>
        </ul>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-sage rounded-xl p-5">
        <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-forest mb-2">
          What Happens Next?
        </h3>
        <ol className="space-y-2 text-sm text-ink-light list-decimal list-inside">
          <li>Your application receives a unique reference number</li>
          <li>Digital verification begins (1-3 days)</li>
          <li>You may be contacted for additional documents</li>
          <li>Physical verification visit is scheduled</li>
          <li>Once fully verified, your campaign goes live</li>
        </ol>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ConsentCheckbox({ checked, onChange, label, description }: any) {
  return (
    <label className="flex items-start gap-3 cursor-pointer group">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        checked={checked}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)}
        className="mt-1 w-5 h-5 rounded border-sage text-emerald-th focus:ring-emerald-th cursor-pointer"
      />
      <div>
        <div className="font-medium text-forest group-hover:text-emerald-th transition-colors">
          {label}
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm text-ink-light mt-0.5">{description}</div>
      </div>
    </label>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { Heart, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { BRAND } from "@/lib/brand";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Logo } from "./layout";

export function Header() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  const navItems = [
    { href: "/patients", label: "Find Patients" },
    { href: "/how-it-works", label: "How It Works" },
    { href: "/about", label: "About Us" },
    { href: "/partners", label: "Corporate Partners" },
  ];

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-cream/80 backdrop-blur-lg border-b border-sage">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between h-16 lg:h-20">
          <Logo />

          {/* Desktop nav */}
          <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1">
            {navItems.map((item) => (
              <Link
                key={item.href}
                href={item.href}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-ink-light hover:text-forest rounded-lg hover:bg-mint transition-colors"
              >
                {item.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
          </nav>

          {/* Desktop CTA */}
          <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-3">
            <Link
              href="/login"
              className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-forest hover:text-forest-dark transition-colors"
            >
              Sign In
            </Link>
            <Link href="/start" className="btn-secondary !py-2.5 !px-5 text-sm">
              Start a Fundraiser
            </Link>
            <Link href="/donate" className="btn-primary !py-2.5 !px-5 text-sm">
              <Heart className="w-4 h-4" />
              Donate Now
            </Link>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile menu button */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)}
            className="lg:hidden p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-mint transition-colors"
            aria-label="Menu"
          >
            {mobileOpen ? <X className="w-6 h-6" /> : <Menu className="w-6 h-6" />}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Mobile menu */}
        {mobileOpen && (
          <div className="lg:hidden py-4 border-t border-sage">
            <nav className="flex flex-col gap-1 mb-4">
              {navItems.map((item) => (
                <Link
                  key={item.href}
                  href={item.href}
                  onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-3 text-base font-medium text-ink hover:bg-mint rounded-lg"
                >
                  {item.label}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </nav>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-4 border-t border-sage">
              <Link href="/login" className="btn-secondary w-full">
                Sign In
              </Link>
              <Link href="/start" className="btn-secondary w-full">
                Start a Fundraiser
              </Link>
              <Link href="/donate" className="btn-primary w-full">
                <Heart className="w-4 h-4" />
                Donate Now
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import { PrismaClient } from "@prisma/client";

const globalForPrisma = globalThis as unknown as {
  prisma: PrismaClient | undefined;
};

export const prisma = globalForPrisma.prisma ?? new PrismaClient();

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production") globalForPrisma.prisma = prisma;

// THANDIZO Demo Data
// All patients, donations and statistics below are CLEARLY LABELLED AS FICTIONAL
// and are used only during development. Real data will replace these once the
// platform is operational and actual applications are received.

import { MedicalCategory } from "@prisma/client";

export const DEMO_DISCLAIMER =
  "DEMO DATA — All patient stories, photographs placeholders and donation figures shown on this development build are illustrative only. They do not represent real individuals or verified fundraising totals.";

export interface DemoCampaign {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  patientInitial: string;
  category: MedicalCategory;
  location: string;
  story: string;
  targetAmount: number;
  raisedAmount: number;
  donorCount: number;
  verificationLevel: "FULL" | "DIGITAL_ONLY" | "PENDING";
  urgency: "EMERGENCY" | "HIGH" | "MEDIUM" | "LOW";
  daysLeft: number;
  gradient: string; // For photo placeholder
}

export const DEMO_CAMPAIGNS: DemoCampaign[] = [
  {
    id: "demo-1",
    slug: "heart-surgery-lusaka-child",
    title: "Heart Surgery for a 6-Year-Old",
    patientInitial: "M",
    category: "SURGERY",
    location: "Lusaka",
    story:
      "A young child requires urgent cardiac surgery that the family cannot afford. The procedure is available at a specialist hospital and time is critical.",
    targetAmount: 185000,
    raisedAmount: 74500,
    donorCount: 142,
    verificationLevel: "FULL",
    urgency: "EMERGENCY",
    daysLeft: 12,
    gradient: "from-emerald-th to-forest",
  },
  {
    id: "demo-2",
    slug: "maternal-care-ndola",
    title: "Safe Delivery & Maternal Care",
    patientInitial: "C",
    category: "MATERNAL_HEALTHCARE",
    location: "Ndola",
    story:
      "An expectant mother with complications needs specialist maternal care and a monitored delivery to ensure both mother and baby are safe.",
    targetAmount: 42000,
    raisedAmount: 31200,
    donorCount: 87,
    verificationLevel: "FULL",
    urgency: "HIGH",
    daysLeft: 28,
    gradient: "from-coral to-gold",
  },

import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export async function createClient() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  return createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() {
          return cookieStore.getAll();
        },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          try {
            cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
              cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
            );
          } catch {
            // The `setAll` method was called from a Server Component.
            // This can be ignored if you have middleware refreshing
            // user sessions.
          }
        },
      },
    }
  );
}

import { createBrowserClient } from "@supabase/ssr";

export function createClient() {
  return createBrowserClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!
  );
}

// Format Zambian Kwacha
export function formatZMW(amount: number): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-ZM", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "ZMW",
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(amount);
}

// Generate application reference number
export function generateReferenceNumber(prefix: string = "THD"): string {
  const year = new Date().getFullYear();
  const random = Math.floor(Math.random() * 999999).toString().padStart(6, "0");
  return `${prefix}-${year}-${random}`;
}

// Time ago formatter
export function timeAgo(date: Date | string): string {
  const d = typeof date === "string" ? new Date(date) : date;
  const seconds = Math.floor((Date.now() - d.getTime()) / 1000);
  if (seconds < 60) return "just now";
  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 60)} minutes ago`;
  if (seconds < 86400) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 3600)} hours ago`;
  if (seconds < 604800) return `${Math.floor(seconds / 86400)} days ago`;
  return d.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" });
}

// THANDIZO Database Schema — Prisma
// This represents the complete data model for the platform.
// Run: npx prisma db push (with DATABASE_URL configured) to create tables.

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

// ============================================================
// USERS & AUTHENTICATION
// ============================================================

enum UserRole {
  PATIENT
  DONOR
  REPRESENTATIVE      // Guardian / patient representative
  CORPORATE_PARTNER
  HOSPITAL_REP
  SUPER_ADMIN
  VERIFICATION_OFFICER
  FINANCIAL_OFFICER
  SUPPORT_OFFICER
}

model User {
  id              String   @id @default(cuid())
  email           String   @unique
  phone           String?  @unique
  passwordHash    String
  fullName        String
  role            UserRole @default(DONOR)
  emailVerified   Boolean  @default(false)
  avatarUrl       String?
  bio             String?
  country         String   @default("ZM")
  createdAt       DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime @updatedAt

  // Relations
  patientProfile    PatientProfile?
  applications      PatientApplication[]
  campaigns         Campaign[]              @relation("CampaignOwner")
  donations         Donation[]              @relation("DonorDonations")
  corporateProfile  CorporatePartner?
  hospitalProfile   HospitalRepresentative?
  verificationCases VerificationCase[]      @relation("AssignedOfficer")
  fieldReports      FieldReport[]
  disbursements     Disbursement[]          @relation("ApprovedBy")
  notifications     Notification[]
  auditLogs         AuditLog[]              @relation("Actor")
  savedCampaigns    SavedCampaign[]
}

// ============================================================
// PATIENTS & APPLICATIONS
// ============================================================

model PatientProfile {
  id              String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId          String   @unique
  user            User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  dateOfBirth     DateTime?
  gender          String?
  nationality     String?
  address         String?
  city            String?
  province        String?
  nationalIdHash  String?  // Never store plaintext IDs
  createdAt       DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime @updatedAt

  applications    PatientApplication[]
}

enum ApplicationStatus {
  DRAFT
  SUBMITTED
  DIGITAL_REVIEW_PENDING
  ADDITIONAL_DOCS_REQUIRED
  DIGITALLY_VERIFIED
  AWAITING_PHYSICAL_VERIFICATION
  PHYSICAL_VERIFICATION_COMPLETED
  FULLY_VERIFIED
  REJECTED
  UNDER_INVESTIGATION
  SUSPENDED
}

model PatientApplication {
  id                    String              @id @default(cuid())
  referenceNumber       String              @unique  // e.g. THD-2026-000123
  userId                String
  user                  User                @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  patientProfileId      String?
  patientProfile        PatientProfile?     @relation(fields: [patientProfileId], references: [id])

  // Medical information
  medicalCondition      String
  diagnosis             String
  situationDescription  String              @db.Text
  hospitalName          String
  hospitalLocation      String?
  attendingDoctor       String?
  procedureRequired     String
  urgency               String              // LOW, MEDIUM, HIGH, EMERGENCY
  estimatedCost         Decimal             @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  currency              String              @default("ZMW")
  additionalNotes       String?             @db.Text

  // Fundraising information
  requestedAmount       Decimal             @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  campaignTitle         String
  patientStory          String              @db.Text
  fundraisingPurpose    String?
  targetDate            DateTime?
  publicPhotoUrl        String?

  // Consent
  medicalDataConsent    Boolean             @default(false)
  identityConsent       Boolean             @default(false)
  publicationConsent    Boolean             @default(false)
  hospitalContactConsent Boolean            @default(false)
  consentTimestamp      DateTime?

  // Status
  status                ApplicationStatus   @default(DRAFT)
  rejectionReason       String?             @db.Text
  submittedAt           DateTime?
  createdAt             DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt             DateTime            @updatedAt

  // Relations
  documents             MedicalDocument[]
  verificationCase      VerificationCase?
  campaign              Campaign?
}

model MedicalDocument {
  id              String              @id @default(cuid())
  applicationId   String
  application     PatientApplication  @relation(fields: [applicationId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  documentType    String              // MEDICAL_REPORT, DOCTOR_LETTER, PRESCRIPTION, INVOICE, LAB_RESULT, ID_DOC, PHOTO, CONSENT
  fileName        String
  fileUrl         String              // Stored in private bucket
  fileSizeBytes   Int
  mimeType        String
  uploadedAt      DateTime            @default(now())
  reviewedBy      String?
  reviewedAt      DateTime?
  reviewNote      String?
}

// ============================================================
// VERIFICATION
// ============================================================

model VerificationCase {
  id                  String              @id @default(cuid())
  applicationId       String              @unique
  application         PatientApplication  @relation(fields: [applicationId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  assignedOfficerId   String?
  assignedOfficer     User?               @relation("AssignedOfficer", fields: [assignedOfficerId], references: [id])

  // Digital verification
  digitalStatus       String              @default("PENDING") // PENDING, IN_PROGRESS, PASSED, FAILED
  identityVerified    Boolean             @default(false)
  medicalDocsVerified Boolean             @default(false)
  hospitalVerified    Boolean             @default(false)
  quotationVerified   Boolean             @default(false)
  digitalNotes        String?             @db.Text
  digitalCompletedAt  DateTime?

  // Physical verification
  physicalStatus      String              @default("NOT_STARTED") // NOT_STARTED, SCHEDULED, IN_PROGRESS, COMPLETED, FAILED
  visitScheduledAt    DateTime?
  hospitalVisited     String?
  contactPerson       String?
  physicalCompletedAt DateTime?

  createdAt           DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime            @updatedAt

  fieldReports        FieldReport[]
}

model FieldReport {
  id                  String            @id @default(cuid())
  verificationCaseId  String
  verificationCase    VerificationCase  @relation(fields: [verificationCaseId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  officerId           String
  officer             User              @relation(fields: [officerId], references: [id])

  visitDate           DateTime
  location            String
  hospitalContacted   Boolean           @default(false)
  contactName         String?
  contactRole         String?
  medicalNeedConfirmed Boolean          @default(false)
  findings            String            @db.Text
  recommendation      String            // APPROVE, REJECT, NEEDS_MORE_INFO
  evidenceUrls        String[]

  submittedAt         DateTime          @default(now())
  createdAt           DateTime          @default(now())
}

// ============================================================
// CAMPAIGNS
// ============================================================

enum CampaignStatus {
  DRAFT
  PENDING_APPROVAL
  ACTIVE
  PAUSED
  COMPLETED
  SUSPENDED
  REJECTED
}

enum MedicalCategory {
  EMERGENCY_TREATMENT
  SURGERY
  CANCER_TREATMENT
  CHILDRENS_HEALTHCARE
  MATERNAL_HEALTHCARE
  MEDICATION
  HOSPITAL_BILLS
  SPECIALIST_TREATMENT
  REHABILITATION
  OTHER
}

model Campaign {
  id                  String            @id @default(cuid())
  slug                String            @unique
  applicationId       String?           @unique
  application         PatientApplication? @relation(fields: [applicationId], references: [id])
  ownerId             String
  owner               User              @relation("CampaignOwner", fields: [ownerId], references: [id])

  title               String
  story               String            @db.Text
  category            MedicalCategory
  publicPhotoUrl      String?
  hospitalName        String?
  location            String?

  targetAmount        Decimal           @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  raisedAmount        Decimal           @db.Decimal(12, 2) @default(0)
  currency            String            @default("ZMW")
  donorCount          Int               @default(0)
  targetDate          DateTime?

  status              CampaignStatus    @default(DRAFT)
  isVerified          Boolean           @default(false)
  verificationLevel   String?           // DIGITAL_ONLY, FULL

  createdAt           DateTime          @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime          @updatedAt
  launchedAt          DateTime?
  completedAt         DateTime?

  donations           Donation[]
  updates             CampaignUpdate[]
  disbursements       Disbursement[]
  savedBy             SavedCampaign[]
  milestones          TreatmentMilestone[]
}

model CampaignUpdate {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  campaignId  String
  campaign    Campaign  @relation(fields: [campaignId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  title       String
  content     String    @db.Text
  photoUrl    String?
  postedAt    DateTime  @default(now())
}

model TreatmentMilestone {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  campaignId  String
  campaign    Campaign  @relation(fields: [campaignId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  title       String
  description String?   @db.Text
  achievedAt  DateTime  @default(now())
}

model SavedCampaign {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  userId      String
  user        User      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  campaignId  String
  campaign    Campaign  @relation(fields: [campaignId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  savedAt     DateTime  @default(now())

  @@unique([userId, campaignId])
}

// ============================================================
// DONATIONS & FINANCIAL
// ============================================================

enum DonationStatus {
  PENDING
  PROCESSING
  SUCCESSFUL
  FAILED
  REFUNDED
}

enum PaymentMethod {
  AIRTEL_MONEY
  MTN_MONEY
  ZAMTEL_KWACHA
  VISA
  MASTERCARD
  BANK_TRANSFER
  CORPORATE_SPONSORSHIP
  GRANT_ALLOCATION
}

model Donation {
  id                  String          @id @default(cuid())
  reference           String          @unique  // THD-DON-XXXXXX
  campaignId          String
  campaign            Campaign        @relation(fields: [campaignId], references: [id])
  donorId             String?
  donor               User?           @relation("DonorDonations", fields: [donorId], references: [id])

  amount              Decimal         @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  currency            String          @default("ZMW")
  paymentMethod       PaymentMethod
  isAnonymous         Boolean         @default(false)
  donorName           String?         // If not anonymous
  donorEmail          String?
  message             String?         @db.Text
  isRecurring         Boolean         @default(false)

  status              DonationStatus  @default(PENDING)
  providerReference   String?         // External payment reference
  failureReason       String?
  receiptUrl          String?

  createdAt           DateTime        @default(now())
  completedAt         DateTime?
  refundedAt          DateTime?
}

enum LedgerType {
  PATIENT_CAMPAIGN_FUNDS
  CORPORATE_SPONSORSHIP
  GRANT_FUNDING
  PLATFORM_OPERATING_INCOME
  PLATFORM_EXPENSE
  DISBURSEMENT
  REFUND
}

model FinancialLedger {
  id              String      @id @default(cuid())
  type            LedgerType
  referenceId     String?     // Donation ID, disbursement ID, grant ID, etc.
  amount          Decimal     @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  currency        String      @default("ZMW")
  description     String
  campaignId      String?
  recordedAt      DateTime    @default(now())
  recordedBy      String?
}

enum DisbursementStatus {
  REQUESTED
  UNDER_REVIEW
  APPROVED
  PROCESSING
  COMPLETED
  REJECTED
  FAILED
}

model Disbursement {
  id                  String              @id @default(cuid())
  reference           String              @unique
  campaignId          String
  campaign            Campaign            @relation(fields: [campaignId], references: [id])

  amount              Decimal             @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  currency            String              @default("ZMW")
  recipientType       String              // HOSPITAL, PATIENT, REPRESENTATIVE
  recipientName       String
  recipientAccountRef String?             // Masked account reference

  status              DisbursementStatus  @default(REQUESTED)
  approvedById        String?
  approvedBy          User?               @relation("ApprovedBy", fields: [approvedById], references: [id])
  approvedAt          DateTime?
  proofOfPaymentUrl   String?
  paymentReference    String?

  requestedAt         DateTime            @default(now())
  completedAt         DateTime?
  rejectionReason     String?
}

// ============================================================
// CORPORATE PARTNERS
// ============================================================

enum PartnershipTier {
  COMMUNITY_SUPPORTER
  SILVER
  GOLD
  PLATINUM
}

enum PartnershipStatus {
  APPLIED
  UNDER_REVIEW
  ACTIVE
  PAUSED
  TERMINATED
}

model CorporatePartner {
  id                  String            @id @default(cuid())
  userId              String            @unique
  user                User              @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  companyName         String
  registrationNumber  String?
  industry            String?
  website             String?
  contactPerson       String
  contactEmail        String
  contactPhone        String?
  logoUrl             String?
  description         String?           @db.Text
  csrFocus            String?           @db.Text

  tier                PartnershipTier   @default(COMMUNITY_SUPPORTER)
  status              PartnershipStatus @default(APPLIED)
  totalContributed    Decimal           @db.Decimal(14, 2) @default(0)
  currency            String            @default("ZMW")

  appliedAt           DateTime          @default(now())
  approvedAt          DateTime?

  sponsorships        Sponsorship[]
}

model Sponsorship {
  id                  String            @id @default(cuid())
  partnerId           String
  partner             CorporatePartner  @relation(fields: [partnerId], references: [id])
  campaignId          String?
  category            MedicalCategory?
  amount              Decimal           @db.Decimal(12, 2)
  currency            String            @default("ZMW")
  purpose             String?           @db.Text
  donationId          String?           // Links to the donation record
  createdAt           DateTime          @default(now())
}

// ============================================================
// GRANTS
// ============================================================

enum GrantStatus {
  OPPORTUNITY_IDENTIFIED
  APPLICATION_DRAFT
  APPLICATION_SUBMITTED
  UNDER_REVIEW
  AWARDED
  REJECTED
  ACTIVE
  COMPLETED
}

enum GrantAllocation {
  PATIENT_ASSISTANCE
  PLATFORM_OPERATIONS
  MIXED
}

model Grant {
  id                  String        @id @default(cuid())
  funderName          String
  funderType          String?       // NGO, FOUNDATION, GOVERNMENT, CORPORATE
  grantName           String
  amount              Decimal       @db.Decimal(14, 2)
  currency            String        @default("ZMW")
  allocation          GrantAllocation @default(PATIENT_ASSISTANCE)

  status              GrantStatus   @default(OPPORTUNITY_IDENTIFIED)
  applicationDeadline DateTime?
  awardDate           DateTime?
  startDate           DateTime?
  endDate             DateTime?

  agreementUrl        String?
  notes               String?       @db.Text

  createdAt           DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt           DateTime      @updatedAt
}

// ============================================================
// HOSPITALS
// ============================================================

enum HospitalStatus {
  APPLIED
  VERIFIED
  ACTIVE
  SUSPENDED
}

model Hospital {
  id                  String                @id @default(cuid())
  name                String
  registrationNumber  String?
  address             String
  city                String
  province            String
  country             String                @default("ZM")
  phone               String?
  email               String?
  website             String?
  documentationUrl    String?

  status              HospitalStatus        @default(APPLIED)
  verifiedAt          DateTime?

  createdAt           DateTime              @default(now())
  representatives     HospitalRepresentative[]
}

model HospitalRepresentative {
  id                  String        @id @default(cuid())
  userId              String        @unique
  user                User          @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  hospitalId          String
  hospital            Hospital      @relation(fields: [hospitalId], references: [id])
  position            String
  verifiedAt          DateTime?
  createdAt           DateTime      @default(now())
}

// ============================================================
// NOTIFICATIONS & AUDIT
// ============================================================

enum NotificationType {
  APPLICATION_UPDATE
  VERIFICATION_UPDATE
  DOCUMENT_REQUEST
  CAMPAIGN_APPROVAL
  DONATION_RECEIVED
  MILESTONE_REACHED
  DISBURSEMENT_UPDATE
  SPONSORSHIP_RECEIVED
  HOSPITAL_CONFIRMATION
  SYSTEM
}

model Notification {
  id          String            @id @default(cuid())
  userId      String
  user        User              @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  type        NotificationType
  title       String
  message     String
  linkUrl     String?
  read        Boolean           @default(false)
  createdAt   DateTime          @default(now())
}

model AuditLog {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  actorId     String?
  actor       User?     @relation("Actor", fields: [actorId], references: [id])
  action      String    // e.g. "APPLICATION_APPROVED", "DONATION_REFUNDED"
  entityType  String    // e.g. "PatientApplication", "Donation"
  entityId    String?
  metadata    Json?
  ipAddress   String?
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
}
